# mawar-adventures-002: campaign interaction extraction

30 scenes · 18 NPCs · 8 items · 89 records

Sources: [transcript](../../../mawar/mawar-adventures-002/cleaned/mawar-adventures-002-source-cleaned.md) · [session_notes](../../../../Campaigns/Mawar%20Adventures/Episodes/Mawar%20Adventures%20Episode%2002.md) · segmentation revision 2

## Review notes

- Version 4 includes documented events beyond the recording: Sefa, lair treasure and restitution, August 6 identification, and August 9 departure. Each note-backed claim links to an exact passage in the original session note; no transcript UIDs are invented.
- Reliah’s visit record now includes the session note’s affirmative account that the party inquires with both sisters. Samar retains the transcript dialogue; no speech is invented for Reliah. Sulfi still answers through a servant.
- Scenes 1–25 retain their indexes and transcript boundaries. The notes add a lair combat before the recovery (formerly scene 26, now 27), and later events; segmentation revision increases to 2.
- The note says Sefa is found dead, then describes restitution “to Sefa” and families of the dead. Preserve that wording as an unresolved recipient ambiguity; do not imply Sefa survived or personally received payment. Payment time and precise location are unspecified.
- The departure is a documented event; the note does not establish that the PCs witnessed a farewell. Its observed records explicitly use session-record perspective.
- One visit to Yaz remains one interaction across arrival, the roof, a sunset pause, and conversation. Ovina has two genuine separate visits plus dawn presence; the afternoon return is not merged across the intervening party split.
- Blue is Ryu’s named companion, not the Silver Tempests PC of the same name. Later combat ASR shifts between Blue, chirk and Turkey; retain explicit presence without claiming that a specific later boat-moving action belongs to Blue.
- Keep the distinct servant, market woman and lightning victim because each has a specific consequential role. Exclude generic drowned combatants, crowds, unnamed mass victims, and the hypothetical future guest-PC introduction. No individual record for every monster or rumor.
- Pull quotes are exact contiguous source text, including awkward ASR. No quote is forced for nonspeaking Blue, the lightning victim, or characters only discussed. Jaeson’s question, Samar’s reply, Benat’s family appeal, Ovina’s reputation, the servant’s refusal, Yaz’s ocean observation, and Azar’s hope are selected.
- All scenes retain source boundaries even when they have no eligible NPC interaction. Minor PC interjections and mechanical discussion remain within whole conversation ranges.

## Azar the Lost · NPC

The party traces, confronts and defeats Azar, hears how the knife exploited his hope of restoring his family, and the notes later record his departure with Benat to heal.

Identity: [Azar the Lost](../../../../People/Mawarans/Azar%20the%20Lost.md)

> There are many lost dead. He can bring them back. I just need a little more time.

Evidence excerpt: [Session note L65–L65](../../../../Campaigns/Mawar%20Adventures/Episodes/Mawar%20Adventures%20Episode%2002.md?plain=1#L65)

> Seeking more information, the party fans out across [[Hamri]] for local witnesses. [[Ryu]] and [[Trok]] speak with [[Jaeson]], while the others inquire with [[Samar]] and [[Reliah]] in the Drowned Flats and [[Orden]] by the docks. Their accounts build a profile of a worn older man who appeared in recent weeks, had been living in or near the tower, left fish remains and the smell of [[Gatza|gatza]], may connect to the Drowned Flats deaths, and had been asking around about lead. After a morning of chasing rumors, the party reconvenes at the market before lunch.

The decoded scroll guides the investigation into Azar’s experiments, Airion’s magic, Nimessa’s service, and the missing people.

Identity: [The Ciphered Scroll](../../../../Campaigns/Mawar%20Adventures/Notes/The%20Ciphered%20Scroll.md)

## Blood Knife · ITEM

The party traces Azar’s murders to the speaking knife, recovers and seals it in lead, but Wazir’s subsequent identification cannot resolve its mystery.

Identity: [Blood Knife](../../../../Things/Magic%20Items/Blood%20Knife.md)

## Ovina’s lead-lined box · ITEM

The party investigates Azar’s commissioned lead-lined box and uses it to contain the recovered knife, which remains locked inside during Wazir’s later identification attempt.

## Sefa · NPC

The party finds Sefa dead in Azar’s lair; the notes also associate restitution with her, without clarifying the recipient after her death.

Identity: [Sefa](../../../../People/Mawarans/Sefa.md)

### Scenes 27 · observed · supporting

The party finds Sefa dead while searching Azar’s lair.

Documented interaction: [Session note L75–L75](../../../../Campaigns/Mawar%20Adventures/Episodes/Mawar%20Adventures%20Episode%2002.md?plain=1#L75)

Evidence excerpt: [Session note L75–L75](../../../../Campaigns/Mawar%20Adventures/Episodes/Mawar%20Adventures%20Episode%2002.md?plain=1#L75)

> That evening, the party searches the [[Splinters]], quickly destroys two more drowned zombies, and finds [[Sefa]] dead in the lair.

### Scenes 28 · mentioned · cameo

The notes name Sefa in the restitution account, without explaining who receives payment on her behalf after her death.

Documented interaction: [Session note L75–L75](../../../../Campaigns/Mawar%20Adventures/Episodes/Mawar%20Adventures%20Episode%2002.md?plain=1#L75)

Evidence excerpt: [Session note L75–L75](../../../../Campaigns/Mawar%20Adventures/Episodes/Mawar%20Adventures%20Episode%2002.md?plain=1#L75)

> From the lair, they recover [[Airion's Elemental Research|three annotated books from Airion's library]], a [[Airion's Bowl of Commanding Water Elementals|brass bowl]], and scattered coins that are paid as restitution to [[Sefa]] and the families of the dead who can be found.
